- **polish-repo-community**: 1. Zero open issues is a red flag, not a green flag. A healthy project has visible activity. 2. Issue templates should lower friction, not raise it. Welcoming, not bureaucratic. 3. Dev setup must take under 10 minutes or you get zero contributors. 4. "Good first issue" must be genuinely approachable, not "rewrite the parser" labeled as easy. 5. - **portaljs-add-chart**: Add a chart (line, bar, area, pie, or scatter) to a dataset's showcase in a PortalJS portal. Installs recharts, writes a reusable Chart component, and renders it in the showcase Views section. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-chart.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dataset**: Add a dataset (CSV, TSV, JSON, or GeoJSON) to an existing PortalJS portal. Appends an entry to datasets.json so the catalog and showcase render it automatically; routes the data by source (local file vs remote URL) — R2 via Git LFS by default, remote URLs by passthrough. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dataset.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dcat**: Make a PortalJS portal harvestable by national/EU/US open-data portals — emit standards-compliant DCAT catalog feeds (DCAT 2/3, DCAT-AP, DCAT-US, national profiles) in JSON-LD, Turtle, and RDF/XML at build, with autodiscovery and per-profile conformance checking. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dcat.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-geo**: Auto-ingest a geospatial file (GeoJSON, Shapefile, GeoPackage, KML/KMZ, FlatGeobuf, CSV-with-geometry) into a PortalJS portal — on your own machine, no server. Normalizes CRS to EPSG:4326, derives a PMTiles render tier + a GeoParquet query tier, pushes all three to R2 via Git LFS, and emits one dual-tier datasets.json entry the showcase renders with &lt;MapPreview&gt; + &lt;GeoQuery&gt;. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-geo.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-map**: Render a GeoJSON dataset on an interactive Leaflet map in the Views section of a dataset's showcase. Installs react-leaflet and a Map component, then renders the map for the chosen dataset. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-map.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-resource**: Add another file (resource) to an EXISTING dataset in a PortalJS portal — a data dictionary, methodology, or an additional data file. Turns a single-file dataset into a multi-resource one; the showcase renders a section per resource. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-resource.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-architect**: Recommend a data-portal architecture (storage, compute, catalog, access, hosting, metadata) from your needs, then hand off to the build skills. The advisory entry point. - **portaljs-check-data-quality**: Audit a local or remote tabular file (CSV/TSV) for common data quality issues. Read-only. Use only when the user explicitly asks to check or audit data quality. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-check-data-quality.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-connect-ckan**: Wire a scaffolded PortalJS portal to a CKAN backend over its API. - **portaljs-migrate**: Migrate (harvest) datasets between open-data platforms. Reads CKAN, a DCAT-US /data.json catalog (DKAN, ArcGIS Hub, data.gov), a DCAT / DCAT-AP RDF feed (JSON-LD, Turtle, or RDF/XML — data.europa.eu, national DCAT-AP portals, GeoDCAT-AP), Socrata, OpenDataSoft, or an ArcGIS FeatureServer, and writes them to a static PortalJS catalog (datasets.json, link-by-URL or download data files into Cloudflare R2 via Git LFS / Giftless) or pushes them into a CKAN instance over its API. - **power.rs**: `set_prevent_sleep(enabled)` / `get_prevent_sleep()`. macOS: spawns `caffeinate -dims -w &lt;our pid&gt;` — `-w` ties the assertion to the app's lifetime, so a crash/quit can never leave an orphan keeping the machine awake (toggle-off kills the child early). Non-macOS returns Err so the frontend toggle stays off. Child handle lives in the managed `PreventSleepState` (registered in [[lib.rs]]). Frontend ([NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus](https://github.com/NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus/blob/HEAD/.mindflow/mirror/tauri/src-tauri/src/commands/power.rs.md) · ★91)
